# Tuning log

Every change to a strategy's frontmatter, newest last: when, what, why, and who.
Most of what it records is the history of a playbook no longer shipped: the
rules were removed on purpose and are being rebuilt by hand, so an id here
usually has no file beside it. `../README.md` holds the sources they came
from.

- 2026-09-14T16:08Z `optimal-play` added as assumption/assumption (the experiment keeps the ground rule the solver and the session hold every comp to: players play optimally) [claude-code-session]
- 2026-09-14T16:25Z `healing-floor` weight: 1 -> 10 (testing an extreme: healing dominates everything else) [claude-code-session]
- 2026-09-14T16:25Z `heal-ten-times-pool` added as constraint/scored: when=team.size >= 1, bonus=min(team.hps_floor / (10 * max(team.pool_total, 1)), 1) * 10 (the user wants healing pushed to an absurd target to see what the engine does with it) [claude-code-session]
- 2026-09-14T16:34Z `heal-ten-times-pool` removed (the test it served is done; the user asked for the shape instead) [claude-code-session]
- 2026-09-14T16:34Z `three-damage` added as constraint/limit: require=team.damage == 3 (the user fixes the shape at two tanks, three damage, one support for the experiment) [claude-code-session]
- 2026-09-14T16:34Z `one-support` added as constraint/limit: require=team.supports == 1 (the user fixes the shape at two tanks, three damage, one support for the experiment) [claude-code-session]
- 2026-09-14T16:36Z `three-damage`, `one-support`, `healing-floor`, `optimal-play` removed (back to the two-tank limit alone, on the user's word) [claude-code-session]
- 2026-09-14T18:08Z `fliers-need-cover` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=team.hitscan, direction=maximize, weight=1, when=matchup.flyers >= 1 (the second rule of the from-scratch playbook: flying requires coverage) [claude-code-session]
- 2026-09-14T18:08Z `healing-covers-the-team` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=team.hps_floor, direction=maximize, weight=1 (the third rule of the from-scratch playbook: healer coverage) [claude-code-session]
- 2026-09-14T18:44Z `healing-covers-the-team` weight: 1 -> 1.5 (stored from the board's slider) [claude-code-session]
- 2026-09-14T18:48Z `healing-covers-the-team` weight: 1.5 -> 1 (stored from the board's slider) [the board]

- 2026-09-14T18:56Z `optimal-play` added as assumption/assumption (the user asked for the assumption that all players play optimally) [claude-code-session]
- 2026-09-14T19:02Z `open-queue-tanks`, `fliers-need-cover`, `optimal-play` prose trimmed to three sentences (the user's rule: a strategy's description is three sentences at most) [claude-code-session]
- 2026-09-14T21:43Z `counters-with-salt` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=team.coverage_share, direction=maximize, weight=0.5, when=enemy.size >= 1 (the user asked for a heuristic that reads the standard counters data but takes it with a grain of salt) [claude-code-session]

# Tuning log

Every change to a strategy's frontmatter, newest last: when, what, why, and who.

- 2026-09-17T03:13Z `amplified-healing` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=team.heal_amp, direction=maximize, weight=0.5 (On the GOATS line: Ana "could nade her entire team and then double healing output on them, which again means insane healing output because the other healers are again already at 100% healing potential", and the play-of-the-game complaint is a "Bio Nade 4 teammates back from Crit".) [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T03:13Z `amplify-the-damage` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=team.dmg_amp, direction=maximize, weight=1 ("Discord is a game changer for a lot of teams by turning 'hes one!' into 'hes done'", Mercy's value "comes instead from damage boost", and GOATS "has discord and nano boost, in actuality it's not that low on damage as long as target selection is good", and "discord orb is mercy damage boost for the entire team".) [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T03:13Z `amplify-thin-heals` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=team.dmg_amp, direction=maximize, weight=0.75, when=matchup.antiheal_need < world.heal_bench ("Enemy tank is tanky but enemy doesn't have high healing maybe Zen" and "Zen was to pretty much put discord on rein and win the one v one earlier.") [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T03:13Z `answer-more-than-exposed` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=team.net_edges, direction=maximize, weight=1 ("If you're strong against three of them but countered by two you can still get great value if you don't engage with your counters in a way the benefits them.") [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T03:13Z `answer-their-ults` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=matchup.ult_answers, direction=maximize, weight=1, when=matchup.ult_threat >= 600 ("everyone tosses their ults into a gigantic mess with double grav and double bomb where no one dies because everyone gets sound barrier'd and tranced.") [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T03:13Z `antiheal-heavy-heal-line` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=team.antiheal, direction=maximize, weight=1.5, when=matchup.antiheal_need >= world.heal_bench * 1.25 ("Ana gets special consideration for being the only support with a win condition on a cooldown, as hitting a large anti-grenade can be an instant teamfight win" and "Teams with Moira need to be wary of Ana.") [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T03:13Z `area-damage-punishes-grouping` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=team.aoe_count, direction=maximize, weight=0.75 ("Heroes with some mobility and AoE damage are especially good at breaking up bunkered formations, because they can... punish the enemy for being so grouped", and "Ashe's dynamite, riptire, grav, barrage, or anything with splash damage benefits from being tightly grouped up".) [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T03:13Z `armor-shrugs-off-spam` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=team.armor_share, direction=maximize, weight=0.75 ("Banning brig gets rid of armour which makes it more justifiable to pick dps heroes", "Their armour makes getting any signifigant damage nigh impossible", and armor is "most useful against weapons with multiple damage sources like shotguns and sub machine guns".) [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T03:13Z `attackers-bring-engage-tools` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=team.mobility_count, direction=maximize, weight=1.5, when=map.side == 'attack' (Into a defensive hold "You need a character with some mobility, good damage and at least medium range", "I swapped to Winston to break through their lines fast and it gave us a win", and at Eichenwalde's first choke "switching to DVa to fly over the buildings to the point".) [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T03:13Z `ban-proof-answers` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=team.banproof_coverage, direction=maximize, weight=0.75 ("I play damage and my only answer is Sombra but she's usually permabanned" and "a good mauga usually bans Ana and will clear discord before diving in.") [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T03:13Z `bans-target-counters` added as constraint/scored: when=team.coverage >= 1, penalty=(team.coverage - team.banproof_coverage) * 0.5 ("Jayne tried ban/protect in a tier 2/3 tournament and it actually just reinforces the meta by protecting the key heroes and banning counters." and "a good mauga usually bans Ana".) [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T03:13Z `barrier-blunts-hitscan` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=team.barrier_hp, direction=maximize, weight=0.75, when=enemy.hitscan >= 2 ("Any hitscan can also be soft-countered by just having a shield to block them" and against snipers "the most obvious solution is a shield/dm/bubbles/not entirely one-shottable comp.") [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T03:13Z `barrier-eats-ults` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=team.barrier_hp, direction=maximize, weight=0.75, when=matchup.ult_threat >= 600 ("the best counter to a Rein ulti is a Rein barrier" and low ranks need "shields to block dva ults.") [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T03:13Z `barriers-block-burst` added as constraint/scored: when=matchup.heal_vs_burst < 0, bonus=min(team.barrier_count, 2) * 0.75 (Reinhardt's shield is for "blocking cooldowns and burst damage in an effort to extend the duration of Rein's presence in that space", and "Sigma can be your guy to mitigate some quick burst damage omni-directionally".) [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T03:13Z `brawl-maps-reward-durability` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=team.pool_total, direction=maximize, weight=1, when=map.style_top == 'brawl' ("Brawl comps tend to be more durable and deadly at close ranges, so once they do close that distance they tend to be able to win the fight" and "Less high ground, shorter sightlines, and restricted side spaces tend to favor more Brawl-centric close range playstyles".) [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T03:13Z `brawl-on-dive-map` added as constraint/scored: when=map.style_top == 'dive' and team.style_lean == 'brawl', penalty=params.BRAWL_ON_DIVE, params.BRAWL_ON_DIVE=1 ("Map geometry is also a bigger issue for brawl comps than dives, since outside of Sym TP their main sources of mobility don't have any vertical component" and "Of the main tanks, Rein suffers the most in areas where high ground is really important.") [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T03:13Z `brawl-on-poke-map` added as constraint/scored: when=map.style_top == 'poke' and team.style_lean == 'brawl', penalty=params.BRAWL_ON_POKE, params.BRAWL_ON_POKE=1.5 ("Their biggest weakness comes from situations where they aren't able to close the distance, since they lack reliable long range damage" and "point A & B of Gibraltor are awful for Zarya, Moira, and Reaper".) [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T03:13Z `brawl-outsustains` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=team.hps_floor, direction=maximize, weight=1.5, when=team.style_lean == 'brawl' ("brawl is supposed to have ways to deny dive's mobility advantages and outsustain and CC flankers up close" and "Moira works best if you need a lot of heals and your team wants to brawl close together.") [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T03:13Z `brawl-stacks-win-conditions` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=team.dmg_ults, direction=maximize, weight=1, when=team.style_lean == 'brawl' ("If you're playing Brawl, you're going to want a lot of them because you're intending on forcing teamfights often.") [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T03:13Z `break-heavy-barriers` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=team.dps_floor, direction=maximize, weight=1, when=matchup.barrier_need >= 1200 ("Having shield busters on your team when you're Rein/Orisa also helps a lot for forcing the enemy to back off" and damage heroes "like bastion and junkrat that can melt barriers.") [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T03:13Z `bring-a-one-shot` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=team.burst_max, direction=maximize, weight=1 ("Dps : you can carry with snipers and flankers (basically anything that oneshots)", and the four-support comp fails because it has "Too much sustain and not enough burst damage".) [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T03:13Z `bring-map-specialists` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=team.map_specialists, direction=maximize, weight=1, when=map.known == 1 (A four-season study "calculated Delta Win Rate (ΔWR = Map WR − Patch Baseline WR) to measure timeless map affinity", which is this metric, and the older thread already knew "some hero picks that are insanely strong on particular maps".) [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T03:13Z `burst-beats-their-saves` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=matchup.burst_vs_heal, direction=maximize, weight=1.5, when=matchup.chew_time_theirs < 999 ("to get any value as a dps you have to kill people faster then the tanks/healers can react... nothing dies unless your hacking+focusing or your 1 shotting them", and "any long range character that can't one shot someone gains nothing but ult charge".) [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T03:13Z `burst-through-heals` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=matchup.burst_vs_heal, direction=maximize, weight=0.75, when=matchup.antiheal_need >= world.heal_bench ("I can't heal oneshots" and damage picks bring "ways to get kills by bursting through resources.") [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T03:13Z `cc-interrupts-ults` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=team.cc_count, direction=maximize, weight=0.5, when=matchup.ult_threat >= 600 (Orisa has "a hard CC that counters a lot of ults and aggressive plays" and "Landing a sleep on the Reaper that was about to 6 man death blossom your team in a grav" is the play that wins games.) [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T03:13Z `cheap-ultimates-come-often` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=team.ult_cost_mean, direction=minimize, weight=0.5 (Baptiste "can charge his ultimate ludicrously quickly to really build a snowball", and "Winning a fight usually means you get more ult charge, giving you a head start in ult economy".) [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T03:13Z `chew-through-them-first` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=matchup.chew_time_ours, direction=minimize, weight=1, when=matchup.chew_time_ours < 999 ("when 5 ppl all shoot at the same target at the same time… they die really fast", and a low-sustain team "will want the fight to be decided as quickly as possible".) [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T03:13Z `chokes-reward-crowd-control` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=team.cc_count, direction=maximize, weight=1, when=map.style_top == 'brawl' ("Place the wall near chokes or in enclosed spaces to trap the enemy" (100 upvotes) and "Shrine requires melee range combat in small choke points (stairs) to capture the point.") [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T03:13Z `commit-to-one-style` added as constraint/scored: when=team.size >= 4 and team.style_lean == '', penalty=params.HYBRID_PENALTY, params.HYBRID_PENALTY=1.5 ("I'm not really playing Brawl, I'm playing a Brawl hero in a Hybrid comp." and "half the team plays one way and the other doesn't leading to fractured playstyles where no one is helping each other at all.") [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T03:13Z `counterpick-knows-its-maps` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=team.map_strategy_hits, direction=maximize, weight=0.5, when=map.known == 1 ("This is why most high rank main tank mains are expected to be capable of all 3 heroes for the maps they excel on" and a guide request for "Which maps are great for which tanks and why".) [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T03:13Z `counters-get-countered` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=matchup.exposure_share, direction=minimize, weight=0.5 ("GOATs actually has counters it's just that those counters are in turn easy to counter with minimal switches from GOATS." and the meta comp "is less vulnerable to the rock-paper-scissors interactions between heroes".) [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T03:13Z `cycle-faster` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=matchup.tempo_diff, direction=maximize, weight=0.75, when=enemy.size >= 1 ("They have much shorter cooldowns than most supports, which makes them better at contesting angles because if a support is doing it and gets forced out, they have massive downtime.") [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T03:13Z `damage-seals-kills` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=team.dps_floor, direction=maximize, weight=1.5 ("The game will end with both at like 20K healing/sub-3K damage and tank and DPS can't seal any kills because there's not enough pressure to burst people down", and "if both of your supports provide no damage at all, then you are going to struggle to keep up with the enemy team and struggle to secure kills".) [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T03:13Z `defenders-set-deployables` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=team.deployables, direction=maximize, weight=1, when=map.side == 'defense' (A whole thread asks "How do you counter Bastion and Torb in super defensive comps", and a diamond tank prefers Orisa on Numbani defense because "she can hold the high ground with heroes like Soldier 76, Bastion, Torb turret really well".) [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T03:13Z `deployables-die-on-attack` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=team.deployables, direction=minimize, weight=0.5, when=map.side == 'attack' ("Bastion/torb are strong spam heroes but highly unflexible", "Sadly Torb is a prime target to rush down" and, of the static comp, "It's kinda ok on defense in escort/hybrid, but is trash at attacking most of the time.") [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T03:13Z `dive-finds-weakest` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=team.pool_min, direction=maximize, weight=0.75, when=matchup.dive_pressure >= 4 ("When I play a dive tank, I categorize the enemy heroes in terms of diveable, not diveable, and need to avoid" and a comp with "such a fragile backline with such little peel potential" loses to it.) [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T03:13Z `dive-needs-mobility` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=team.mobility_count, direction=maximize, weight=2, when=team.style_lean == 'dive' ("Dive comps utilize individual mobility skills to out-maneuver their opponent" and "If your team plays dive, you just pick a hero that has good movement.") [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T03:13Z `dive-still-needs-heals` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=team.hps_floor, direction=maximize, weight=1, when=team.style_lean == 'dive' ("Surprised how often I get this pairing of no forward healing in a comp with 3 (or 4 in 6v6) dive heroes. So everyone else has to swap around them.") [community-playbook]

[community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T03:13Z `everyone-shoots` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=team.dps_count, direction=maximize, weight=1 ("you want to have at least one support providing some degree of damage pressure. - 2026-09-17T03:13Z `fewer-squishies` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=team.squish_count, direction=minimize, weight=1 ("I just don't think it's enough to have such a fragile backline... once your supports start getting picked off it'll snowball real bad", and from the Reinhardt side "a couple of swings and you delete any squishy".) - 2026-09-17T03:13Z `field-more-hit-points` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=team.pool_total, direction=maximize, weight=0.75 (GOATS held because "there's just lots of sustain and HP with the tanks" and "Tanks have enough HP to survive poke damage without having to totally retreat and be passive".) - 2026-09-17T04:09Z `solo-tank-backline-peels` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=team.cc_count, direction=maximize, weight=1, when=team.tanks <= 1 ("In 6v6 it would be the off-tank's job, (not uniquely but in large part) to peel for supports.") [community-playbook]

- 2026-09-17T04:09Z `solo-tank-needs-armor` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=team.armor_total, direction=maximize, weight=1, when=team.tanks <= 1 ("Orisa was played as a solo tank even before 5v5 with triple DPS because you couldn't focus her down.") [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T04:09Z `solo-tank-solo-heal` added as constraint/scored: when=team.tanks <= 1 and team.supports <= 1 and team.size >= 4, penalty=2 ("In the event that your team is stuck with a solo Tank AND a solo Support, you might as well just go 5th and 6th DPS because your team is pretty much throwing.") [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T04:09Z `splash-answers-tight-core` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=team.aoe_count, direction=maximize, weight=0.5, when=enemy.core_size >= 4 ("Suddenly, there were no more characters who can do damage to several targets at once. I think if Pharah still had her old splash damage ... GOATS would have a counter.") [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T04:09Z `splash-over-barriers` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=team.aoe_count, direction=maximize, weight=0.75, when=enemy.barrier_count >= 2 ("splash antis above enemy shields" and "You can shoot over some barriers, and you force enemies to split their attention/resources between you and your allies on the ground".) [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T04:09Z `stages-want-both-reaches` added as constraint/scored: when=map.stages >= 3, bonus=(0.5 if team.range_max >= params.LONG_GUN else 0) + min(team.melee, 1) * 0.5, params.LONG_GUN=60 ("On Nepal Sanctum, your tank needs to be ranged (Orisa, Sigma, Hog) or mobile enough to both pressure the point and also enemy snipers firing across long sightlines" and "Shrine requires melee range combat in small choke points (stairs) to capture the point".) [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T04:09Z `stall-tools-contest-point` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=team.invuln, direction=maximize, weight=0.5, when=map.stages >= 3 ("I'll legitimately use Hammond to race to a point to stall if it will let my team catch up and keep the point", "Just stall the point and die so you can regroup with your team" and, on overtime ultimates, "Baptise and Kiriko also great for invulnerability abilities".) [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T04:09Z `strength-outlasts-counters` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=team.win_mean, direction=maximize, weight=1, when=enemy.size >= 4 ("if youre still getting value on winston even into reaper zen, its worth playing it out and improving") [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T04:09Z `support-heavy-needs-uptime` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=team.cooldown_median, direction=minimize, weight=1, when=team.supports >= 3 ("What makes DPS better at their job than supports is their uptime. They have much shorter cooldowns than most supports, which makes them better at contesting angles because if a support is doing it and gets forced out, they have massive downtime".) [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T04:09Z `supports-heal-in-chunks` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=team.heal_peak_supports, direction=maximize, weight=1 ("you want one main healer (high burst healing)" and "oftentimes targeted burst healing is what you need to save a critical teammate".) [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T04:09Z `supports-pair-against-dive` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=team.synergy_density, direction=maximize, weight=0.75, when=matchup.dive_pressure >= 4 ("usually supports have tons of options to deal with divers by working together and peeling should be something that only happens if the enemy team is running a full dive comp".) [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T04:09Z `sustained-pressure-baits-saves` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=matchup.chew_time_ours, direction=minimize, weight=1, when=enemy.invuln >= 2 ("a good team will cycle their tzusu and lamp to the point I either bait one ability or waste too mcuh resources" and "baiting his regen and/or lamp is really what you are going for".) [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T04:09Z `symmetrical-leave-deployables` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=team.deployables, direction=minimize, weight=0.5, when=map.known == 1 and map.sided == 0 ("On KOTH or flashpoint you surely don't stay on the objective the entire time" and "In symmetrical modes (KOTH and Push), mobile heroes are vital for ensuring teams can quickly contest strong positions in the neutral map center".) [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T04:09Z `symmetrical-race-centre` added as constraint/scored: when=map.known == 1 and map.sided == 0, bonus=min(team.mobility_count, params.MOBILE_CAP) * 0.5, params.MOBILE_CAP=3 ("In symmetrical modes (KOTH and Push), mobile heroes are vital for ensuring teams can quickly contest strong positions in the neutral map center" and "Damn, flashpoint really punishes low mobility heroes, huh".) [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T04:09Z `synergy-that-answers` added as constraint/scored: when=enemy.size >= 3 and team.coverage_share >= 0.5, bonus=min(team.synergy_edges, params.PAIR_CAP) * 0.5, params.PAIR_CAP=4 ("The Sigma Mizuki comp was primarily used to shut down the Dva comps" and "when the tank is not Diva the comp fell off so hard".) [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T04:09Z `tank-answer-needs-second` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=team.double_covered, direction=maximize, weight=0.75, when=enemy.tanks >= 2 ("she def needs support help to not get overrun once you are out of bubbles" and "He doesn't have a hard counter but needs teamwork with your dps to shutdown".) [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T04:09Z `teamwork-answers-uncounterable` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=team.synergy_score, direction=maximize, weight=1, when=enemy.size >= 1 and team.coverage < enemy.size ("He doesn't have a hard counter but needs teamwork with your dps to shutdown" and "So you either need your teammates to help you counter them".) [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T04:09Z `thin-line-self-heals` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=team.heal_peak_total, direction=maximize, weight=1, when=team.supports <= 1 ("Bap has 350 effective HP with his shift", "With one button press, Ana can both self heal for 100" and "Everyone is responsible for their own health bars".) [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T04:09Z `thin-map-trust-ladder` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=team.win_mean, direction=maximize, weight=1, when=map.known == 1 and team.map_pick_mass < 30 ("The sample size is still rather low so would get more healthy values for reworked maps in 1-2 more seasons.") [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T04:09Z `third-healer-overheals` added as constraint/scored: when=team.supports >= 3 and team.heal_ratio >= params.OVERHEAL, penalty=0.75, params.OVERHEAL=1.5 ("Too much sustain and not enough burst damage.") [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T04:09Z `three-damage-both-tanks` added as constraint/scored: when=team.damage >= 3 and team.tanks < 2, penalty=1.5 ("Then a solo Tank has to figure out how to survive, protect his team, and take ground without the synergy and flexibility of an off-tank backup.") [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T04:09Z `three-damage-three-jobs` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=team.subrole_diversity, direction=maximize, weight=1, when=team.damage >= 3 ("Tip for dps players that haven't heard this before, try not to double up on either 2 hitscans or 2 flex dps.") [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T04:09Z `three-supports-both-tanks` added as constraint/scored: when=team.supports >= 3 and team.tanks < 2, penalty=1.5 ("4 tanks 2 support -> 3+3 was the GOATS meta in OW1, look it up for history.") [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T04:09Z `three-supports-cannot-attack` added as constraint/scored: when=map.side == 'attack' and team.supports >= 3, penalty=params.THIRD_SUPPORT, params.THIRD_SUPPORT=1 ("It's kinda ok on defense in escort/hybrid, but is trash at attacking most of the time" and "it's impossible to break through a choke without the dps passives as well".) - 2026-09-17T04:09Z `three-supports-three-flavours` added as heuristic/heuristic: metric=team.subrole_diversity, direction=maximize, weight=0.5, when=team.supports >= 3 ("You really need to know 2-3 healers and they need to be different flavors.") [community-playbook]
- 2026-09-17T04:09Z `two-barriers-at-most` added as constraint/scored: penalty=max(0, team.barrier_count - 2) * 0.5 ("6v6 is not the double shield pokeslop anymore like in ow1, so adding one tank to each team adds more damage than mitigation most of the time.") [community-playbook]

- 2026-09-17T04:09Z `two-off-map-picks` added as constraint/scored: when=map.known == 1 and team.map_offmap >= params.OFFMAP, penalty=1, params.OFFMAP=2 ("But I still feel you need 2 or 3 hero pool per role because some maps just dont work.") [community-playbook]

- 2026-09-17T04:09Z `two-swingers-no-rate` added as constraint/scored: when=team.rank_sensitive_count >= params.SWINGERS, penalty=1, params.SWINGERS=2 ("People are in general either very good at zen or very bad at zen; he's punishing enough that people don't generally try to force him.")
